<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-49998895" data-id="49998895" data-tags="Science|Culture|Space" data-score="2103" data-comments="251" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998895" data-time="1791407778">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.mit.edu/2026/margaret-hamilton-computing-pioneer-dies-1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Margaret Hamilton has died</span><span class='zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿去世</span></a><span class="dom">news.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Margaret Hamilton, a trailblazing software engineer who led NASA&#x27;s Apollo-era onboard software efforts, has died.</span><span class='zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿作为推动软件工程成为正式学科的先驱，带领团队开发了阿波罗任务的飞行软件，显著提高关键系统的可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Margaret Hamilton, a trailblazing software engineer who led NASA&#x27;s Apollo-era onboard software efforts, has died. Her work helped formalize software engineering as a discipline and improve the reliability of critical flight systems.</span><span class='zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿作为推动软件工程成为正式学科的先驱，带领团队开发了阿波罗任务的飞行软件，显著提高关键系统的可靠性。她的去世让人们回顾这位女性在早期计算领域的重大贡献及其对现代软件的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral AI unveils Mistral Large 4, a new open-weight language model intended to deliver strong performance with broad accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral AI 发布了 Mistral Large 4，面向开源生态的语言模型，强调可获取性与性能平衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral AI unveils Mistral Large 4, a new open-weight language model intended to deliver strong performance with broad accessibility. The release underscores the momentum behind community-driven models as alternatives to big incumbents.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral AI 发布了 Mistral Large 4，面向开源生态的语言模型，强调可获取性与性能平衡。此举反映了开源模型在市场上的持续崛起，成为对抗大型垄断模型的有力选项。</span></p>

It highlights how contemporary tooling and UX can reinvent retro titles for new audiences.</span><span class='zh'>AAA级扫雷将经典益智游戏推向高水准的现代化呈现，拥有精致的美术、流畅的交互和性能优化。该项目展示了当代工具链与用户体验如何为复古游戏注入新活力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare acquires Deno</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 收购 Deno，整合边缘计算生态</span></a><span class="dom">deno.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform. The deal could accelerate edge-based development and broaden Cloudflare’s developer toolkit, though it raises questions about governance and open-source leadership in the workspace.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。此举可能加速基于边缘的开发并扩展 Cloudflare 的开发者工具包，同时也引发关于治理与开源领导力的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dgt.is/blog/2026-10-07-deepseek-freek-out/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why isn&#x27;t the industry freaking out about DeepSeek 4.1 Flash?</span><span class='zh'>为什么业界对 DeepSeek 4.1 闪存不惊慌？</span></a><span class="dom">dgt.is</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post questions the market&#x27;s reaction to DeepSeek 4.1 Flash, perhaps arguing that the technology&#x27;s impact on performance and storage economics is uncertain or anticipated.</span><span class='zh'>作者质疑 DeepSeek 4.1 闪存的行业影响为何未引发广泛震动，认为对性能与存储经济性的实际影响还有不确定之处。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post questions the market&#x27;s reaction to DeepSeek 4.1 Flash, perhaps arguing that the technology&#x27;s impact on performance and storage economics is uncertain or anticipated. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-haiku-5-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Haiku 5.5</span><span class='zh'>Claude Haiku 5.5</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died. He helped popularize accessible, narrative-driven tech journalism with works like I, Cringely and commentary on Apple and Silicon Valley, shaping how mainstream audiences think about the industry. His passing marks the end of an era for journalism that mixes insider storytelling with tech analysis.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。他以 I, Cringely 等作品和对苹果及科技行业的评论，推动了以叙事方式讲述科技的风格，影响了主流对科技产业的理解。他的离世标志着一种将行业分析与个人叙事相结合的新闻传统的告别。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Run Qwen 3.8 Flash Next (125B) on consumer hardware (RTX 4090) at 100T/s</span><span class='zh'>在消费级显卡 RTX 4090 上实现 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B 模型的 100T/s 吞吐</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s. The work leverages optimized kernels and model quantization, illustrating how multi-hundred-billion-parameter models may become feasible on consumer hardware for research and prototyping. This pushes the boundaries of on-device AI, enabling privacy-preserving demos and edge deployments.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。该工作利用优化内核与模型量化，展示了在消费级硬件上运行大模型的可行性，为研究、原型开发以及隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/omlahore/RemoveMacAI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 and get its disk space back</span><span class='zh'>在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 并回收磁盘空间</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components. It highlights the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and feature completeness on future macOS builds.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。此举揭示了系统级 AI 功能对存储与性能的影响，以及未来 macOS 构建中的取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rea.tools/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>REA Reverse – Engineer Anything</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse：逆向工程万物</span></a><span class="dom">rea.tools</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work.</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work. It aims to democratize RE skills by curating practical content and community-driven exercises. This matters for defenders, researchers, and hardware hackers looking to deepen code comprehension and vulnerability analysis.</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。通过实操内容和社区驱动的练习，推动逆向工程技能的普及。对安全研究、漏洞分析和硬件黑客都具有现实意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://htmx.org/essays/yes-and/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Yes, and</span><span class='zh'>Yes, and（是的，而且）</span></a><span class="dom">htmx.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay argues for additive collaboration in software, embracing &#x27;Yes, and&#x27; as a mindset rather than hesitation.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张在软件开发中采用“Yes, and”作为协作思维，强调在不否定对方想法的前提下叠加改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay argues for additive collaboration in software, embracing &#x27;Yes, and&#x27; as a mindset rather than hesitation. Using HTMX as a practical lens, it shows how to build incrementally while preserving compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张在软件开发中采用“Yes, and”作为协作思维，强调在不否定对方想法的前提下叠加改进。以 HTMX 为例，展示如何在保持向后兼容的同时实现逐步扩展。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A YouTuber says police visited him after he built a Flock-style camera to track cops. It raises questions about privacy, safety, and accountability when DIY cameras monitor public spaces.</span><span class='zh'>网红称警方在他自制的Flock风格摄像头用于追踪警察后上门。此事暴露了民用监控工具与执法之间的紧张关系，也引发对隐私和安全的讨论。它强调在 DIY 摄像头监控公共场域时，谁应对行为负责以及相关法律边界的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bigwords.page/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Bigwords.page – Turn any screen into a sign. The URL is the app</span><span class='zh'>Bigwords.page：把任意屏幕变成标牌，URL 就是应用</span></a><span class="dom">bigwords.page</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bigwords.page turns any URL into a shareable oversized sign displayed on a screen, effectively turning the URL itself into the app.</span><span class='zh'>Bigwords.page 把任意链接变成可在屏幕上显示的超大标牌，URL 本身即为应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bigwords.page turns any URL into a shareable oversized sign displayed on a screen, effectively turning the URL itself into the app. It&#x27;s a lightweight, browser-based signage tool suitable for events or quick alerts.</span><span class='zh'>Bigwords.page 把任意链接变成可在屏幕上显示的超大标牌，URL 本身即为应用。这个轻量的浏览器工具适合活动现场或快速发布通知。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mathstodon.xyz/@tao/117395269325940185" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“Math 2.0” will need to value mathematical progress more holistically</span><span class='zh'>“Math 2.0”将以更全面的标准评估数学进步</span></a><span class="dom">mathstodon.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Math 2.0 advocates rethinking how mathematical progress is measured, moving beyond traditional exams.</span><span class='zh'>Math 2.0主张重新定义数学进步的衡量标准，超越传统考试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Math 2.0 advocates rethinking how mathematical progress is measured, moving beyond traditional exams. It emphasizes holistic indicators such as conceptual understanding, problem-solving, collaboration, and real-world impact, which could reshape curricula and assessment practices.</span><span class='zh'>Math 2.0主张重新定义数学进步的衡量标准，超越传统考试。它强调概念理解、问题解决、协作与实际应用等全面指标，认为这将影响课程设计与评估方式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>603</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002008' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>638</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49991852" data-id="49991852" data-tags="Design|Programming|Science" data-score="603" data-comments="115" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991852" data-time="1791376240">
<a class="thumb" href="https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/social-card.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='patkerr.co.uk' data-c='#f472b6' data-tag='Design'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">25</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>2D Vehicles</span><span class='zh'>2D 车辆系统</span></a><span class="dom">patkerr.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction. It emphasizes balancing realism with a lightweight engine, showing how small tweaks can dramatically affect handling.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。作者平衡真实感与简化，展示微小物理参数的调整如何影响轻量级引擎中的操控性与性能。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector. His contributions helped establish high-energy neutrinos as a major new window on the cosmos, enabling discoveries about distant cosmic sources.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。他的工作把高能中微子确立为研究宇宙的新窗口，推动了对遥远宇宙源头的理解。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://developer.chrome.com/blog/jpeg-xl-in-chrome" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shipping JPEG XL in Chrome</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 将原生支持 JPEG XL</span></a><span class="dom">developer.chrome.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 已原生支持 JPEG XL，推动网页图像压缩进入新阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web. JPEG XL offers higher quality at smaller file sizes and supports features like animation and HDR, which could reduce bandwidth and streamline asset pipelines for publishers and developers.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 已原生支持 JPEG XL，推动网页图像压缩进入新阶段。JPEG XL 在更小尺寸下提供更高画质，并支持动画与 HDR，有望降低带宽、简化资产工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982498" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: GitHub refuses to remove cracked copies of my software after a month</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 拒绝在一个月后移除我的破解软件副本</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation.</span><span class='zh'>开发者指出尽管提出下架请求，GitHub 仍未清除其软件的破解副本，暴露了执法与平台治理之间的矛盾及版权保护的难题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation. The piece underscores the ongoing challenge creators face in maintaining control over distributed software.</span><span class='zh'>开发者指出尽管提出下架请求，GitHub 仍未清除其软件的破解副本，暴露了执法与平台治理之间的矛盾及版权保护的难题。文章强调创作者在分发软件时维权的持续挑战。</span></p>

The incident underscores government data security risks and the need for stronger access controls, auditing, and breach response measures.</span><span class='zh'>未经授权的访问导致约880万丹麦公民的个人信息被曝光（包括 CPR 编号等）。此事件凸显政府数据安全的脆弱性，强调需要加强访问控制、审计与事件响应。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents in the Opus framework autonomously discovered two candidate room-temperature magnetic semiconductors, pushing spintronics closer to practical devices.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 框架中的 AI 代理自动发现两种室温磁性半导体候选材料，为自旋电子学向实用器件推进带来希望。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents in the Opus framework autonomously discovered two candidate room-temperature magnetic semiconductors, pushing spintronics closer to practical devices. This showcases how AI-assisted materials discovery can accelerate breakthroughs that once seemed out of reach.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 框架中的 AI 代理自动发现两种室温磁性半导体候选材料，为自旋电子学向实用器件推进带来希望。这体现了 AI 辅助的材料发现能显著加速原本难以实现的突破。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Polars 2.0 brings major performance and API improvements to the fast DataFrame library, with enhancements to lazy evaluation, memory usage, and language bindings. The release tightens competition in data tooling for Python and Rust, appealing to data engineers who need speed and scalability.</span><span class='zh'>Polars 2.0 为高性能 DataFrame 库带来重大的性能与 API 改进，提升延迟求值、内存利用率与语言绑定。此版本增强了 Python 与 Rust 的数据处理工作流的速度与可扩展性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://beaksec.github.io/posts/telegram-desktop-one-click-account-takeover/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Telegram Desktop vulnerability allowed any user&#x27;s file to be stolen</span><span class='zh'>Telegram 桌面端漏洞致使任意用户文件可能被窃取</span></a><span class="dom">beaksec.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Telegram Desktop suffered a vulnerability that let an attacker access and steal a victim&#x27;s files via the desktop client, potentially enabling a one-click account takeover.</span><span class='zh'>Telegram 桌面端存在漏洞，攻击者可通过桌面客户端访问并窃取受害者的文件，甚至实现一键式账户接管。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Telegram Desktop suffered a vulnerability that let an attacker access and steal a victim&#x27;s files via the desktop client, potentially enabling a one-click account takeover. The bug highlights how trusted desktop apps can become backdoors for data exfiltration, so users should update to patched versions and developers should harden local storage and permission handling.</span><span class='zh'>Telegram 桌面端存在漏洞，攻击者可通过桌面客户端访问并窃取受害者的文件，甚至实现一键式账户接管。这一漏洞凸显受信任的桌面应用可能成为数据外泄的后门，用户应尽快升级打补丁，开发者需加强本地存储与权限管理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnbc.com/2026/10/08/open-ai-revenue-nvidia-oracle-coreweave.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI annualised revenues $20B less than previously signalled</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 年化收入比此前预计低出200亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">cnbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI reported annualized revenue that came in about $20B below what investors had signaled, signaling a sharper slowdown or slower-than-expected monetization of its model.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的年化收入约比此前透露的数字低出约200亿美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI reported annualized revenue that came in about $20B below what investors had signaled, signaling a sharper slowdown or slower-than-expected monetization of its model. The shortfall highlights how even top-tier AI platforms are still iterating on pricing, enterprise adoption, and cost-to-revenue dynamics as competition and infrastructure costs mount.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的年化收入约比此前透露的数字低出约200亿美元。该修正透露出其商业化路径的不确定性，以及企业客户的实际 uptake 以及竞争与基础设施成本对盈利的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EmbeddingGemma 2 builds on Google&#x27;s open Gemma line with a lightweight, multimodal embedding model designed for fast retrieval across text and vision.</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 在开放的 Gemma 基础上，提供一个轻量级的多模态嵌入模型，面向快速跨模态检索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EmbeddingGemma 2 builds on Google&#x27;s open Gemma line with a lightweight, multimodal embedding model designed for fast retrieval across text and vision. By staying open and small, it lowers barriers for researchers and developers to prototype cross-modal apps and run experiments on modest hardware.</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 在开放的 Gemma 基础上，提供一个轻量级的多模态嵌入模型，面向快速跨模态检索。保持开源和体积小，降低研究者和开发者在中等硬件上原型化跨模态应用的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/franzenzenhofer/big-arrow-on-the-screen" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Let your AI agents paint big arrows, boxes and text on your screen</span><span class='zh'>让 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A lightweight demo shows AI agents annotating the UI with large arrows, boxes, and labels to explain their plans in real time.</span><span class='zh'>一个轻量级演示展示 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本标签，以实时解释其计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A lightweight demo shows AI agents annotating the UI with large arrows, boxes, and labels to explain their plans in real time. Such overlays boost interpretability and debugging, helping humans understand where the agent is headed and why. The approach is easy to prototype and could be used in demos, tutorials, or human-in-the-loop workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个轻量级演示展示 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本标签，以实时解释其计划。此类覆盖能提升可解释性与调试效果，帮助人类理解代理的意图与下一步动作。该方法易于原型化，适用于演示、教程和人机协作场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cphpost.dk/2026-10-10/news/round-up/123456-password-used-in-massive-danish-cpr-data-breach/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>`123456&#x27; password used in Danish CPR data breach</span><span class='zh'>丹麦 CPR 数据泄露中使用了 123456 密码</span></a><span class="dom">cphpost.dk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Attackers used the simple password 123456 to access Danish CPR records, exposing sensitive personal data.</span><span class='zh'>攻击者使用简单密码 123456 访问了丹麦 CPR 记录，暴露了敏感个人信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Attackers used the simple password 123456 to access Danish CPR records, exposing sensitive personal data. This incident shows how weak credentials can enable large scale government data breaches and underscores the need for stronger authentication and password hygiene across public-facing systems.</span><span class='zh'>攻击者使用简单密码 123456 访问了丹麦 CPR 记录，暴露了敏感个人信息。该事件凸显了弱密码如何使政府数据大规模泄露成为可能，也强调在面向公众的系统中需要更强的身份认证和密码管理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://quesma.com/blog/invisible-cities-one-shot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I gave Opus 5.5 one prompt and six hours to visualize Invisible Cities</span><span class='zh'>给 Opus 5.5 一个提示，花六小时可视化《隐形城市》</span></a><span class="dom">quesma.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A single prompt and hours-long iteration to coax Opus 5.5 to visualize Italo Calvino&#x27;s &#x27;Invisible Cities&#x27; reveals how modern AI handles abstract literature.</span><span class='zh'>用一个提示、持续数小时的迭代，试图让 Opus 5.5 将意大利作家卡维诺的《隐形城市》变为可视画面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A single prompt and hours-long iteration to coax Opus 5.5 to visualize Italo Calvino&#x27;s &#x27;Invisible Cities&#x27; reveals how modern AI handles abstract literature. The experiment highlights both the model&#x27;s creative potential and its limits in mapping poetic descriptions to visuals, with time and prompt design driving the result.</span><span class='zh'>用一个提示、持续数小时的迭代，试图让 Opus 5.5 将意大利作家卡维诺的《隐形城市》变为可视画面。结果揭示了当前多模态模型在将抽象文学转化为视觉表现方面的潜力与局限性，提示设计与时间投入对输出影响显著。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/szabadkai/c64-keyboard-font/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A font recreated from photographs of classic Commodore 64 keycaps</span><span class='zh'>从经典 Commodore 64 键帽照片重现的字体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design.</span><span class='zh'>一个字体完全由经典 Commodore 64 键帽的照片重建而成，展示了通过摄影测量和图像方法推动字体设计的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/psychiatry/articles/10.3389/fpsyt.2025.1697900/full" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ADHD as a circadian rhythm disorder: evidence and implications for chronotherapy (2025)</span><span class='zh'>ADHD 被视为昼夜节律障碍：证据与时序治疗的启示（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">frontiersin.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New evidence positions ADHD as a circadian rhythm disorder, with implications for chronotherapy strategies.</span><span class='zh'>新证据将 ADHD 定位为昼夜节律障碍，可能改变时序治疗策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New evidence positions ADHD as a circadian rhythm disorder, with implications for chronotherapy strategies. Timing medications and light exposure could help align symptoms and improve outcomes, signaling a shift toward circadian-aware care.</span><span class='zh'>新证据将 ADHD 定位为昼夜节律障碍，可能改变时序治疗策略。通过优化药物时机和光照暴露，或能提升治疗效果，推动以昼夜节律为核心的治疗模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://community.bitwarden.com/t/published-version-update-in-app-stores/102750" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bitwarden Dual License Model</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden 双许可证模式</span></a><span class="dom">community.bitwarden.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bitwarden introduces a dual-licensing model to balance openness with commercial viability, offering an AGPL-licensed community edition alongside a paid license for enterprise deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden 推出双许可证模式，在社区版采用 AGPL 的同时提供企业级付费许可，以兼顾开源透明度与商业可行性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bitwarden introduces a dual-licensing model to balance openness with commercial viability, offering an AGPL-licensed community edition alongside a paid license for enterprise deployments. This approach aims to keep core software open while providing a compliant path for large organizations to deploy Bitwarden at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden 推出双许可证模式，在社区版采用 AGPL 的同时提供企业级付费许可，以兼顾开源透明度与商业可行性。此举旨在为大型组织的部署提供合规路径，同时确保核心开源工作继续受益于社区贡献。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Decisions API is in public beta, enabling developers to encode multi-step decision making into apps using the model’s planning and recommendation capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Decisions API 现处公开测试版，允许开发者在应用中嵌入多步决策能力，利用模型的规划与推荐功能输出选项、权衡与建议行动，并可对结果设定约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Decisions API is in public beta, enabling developers to encode multi-step decision making into apps using the model’s planning and recommendation capabilities. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/10/i-paid-people-to-try-and-follow-my-readme/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I paid people to try and follow my README</span><span class='zh'>我花钱让人尝试按我的 README 操作</span></a><span class="dom">shkspr.mobi</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author paid participants to try following his README, turning documentation into a live test. It offers practical lessons on writing clearer, testable onboarding docs.</span><span class='zh'>作者花钱让人尝试按他的 README 做事，将文档变成一次真实测试。结果暴露出真实用户在执行步骤时遇到的歧义和阻碍，凸显文档传达中的不足。为编写更清晰、可测试的上手文档提供了可操作的经验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/boykopovar/AnyPS5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AnyPS5: Port PS5 binaries to PC without emulation (87% system libraries mapped)</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5：在不使用模拟的情况下将 PS5 二进制移植到 PC（87% 系统库已映射）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation. The project showcases progress in cross-architecture porting but raises legal, licensing, and anti-tampering questions as it matures.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。该项目展示了跨架构移植的进展，但随着成熟，也引发法律、授权和反篡改等方面的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ircv3.net/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IRCv3</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 规范</span></a><span class="dom">ircv3.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers. It aims to make IRC more usable and interoperable in a world of feature-rich clients. The spec underpins improvements like extended joins and better identity handling.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。它旨在让 IRC 在功能丰富的客户端时代更易用、互操作性更强。该规范支撑了扩展加入、身份处理等改进。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI has withdrawn three math papers from its official archive, citing the need for corrections or updates.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已从其数学论文存档撤下三篇论文，原因在于需要纠正或更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI has withdrawn three math papers from its official archive, citing the need for corrections or updates. The withdrawals highlight ongoing scrutiny of rigor and reproducibility in AI-aligned mathematical research, and point to the importance of versioning and clear disclosures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已从其数学论文存档撤下三篇论文，原因在于需要纠正或更新。此举凸显了围绕 AI 相关数学研究的严格性与可复现性持续受关注，强调版本控制和明确披露的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phys.org/news/2026-10-nature-capacity-species-lost-vastly.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nature&#x27;s capacity to &#x27;bounce back&#x27; when species are lost is overestimated: study</span><span class='zh'>物种灭绝后自然界的恢复能力被严重高估</span></a><span class="dom">phys.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New research shows nature&#x27;s capacity to recover after species loss is vastly overestimated; simulations reveal that ecosystems may undergo slow recovery or irreversible shifts, with cascading effects.</span><span class='zh'>新研究表明，自然界在物种流失后恢复的能力被严重高估；模拟显示生态系统可能缓慢恢复或发生不可逆转的转变，产生连锁效应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New research shows nature&#x27;s capacity to recover after species loss is vastly overestimated; simulations reveal that ecosystems may undergo slow recovery or irreversible shifts, with cascading effects. The finding implies conservation strategies should prioritize preventing losses of keystone species and maintaining network robustness.</span><span class='zh'>新研究表明，自然界在物种流失后恢复的能力被严重高估；模拟显示生态系统可能缓慢恢复或发生不可逆转的转变，产生连锁效应。该发现提醒在保护策略中应优先防止关键物种流失，维护生态网络的韧性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new paper examines how Navier–Stokes equations are translated into discrete algorithms and points out potential pitfalls that can lead to inaccurate or unstable simulations.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文探讨将 Navier–Stokes 方程转化为数值算法时可能出现的问题，揭示数值建模中的潜在误差与稳定性挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FeSens/openTPU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenTPU – An open-source AI accelerator, developed by AI</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU：一个开源的AI加速器项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures. By inviting community contributions, it aims to democratize access to AI hardware and accelerate experimentation beyond vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。通过鼓励社区贡献，它旨在降低对厂商锁定的依赖，推动 AI 硬件的普及与创新。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions. It connects retro media interfaces to modern UX thinking, arguing that design quality can still inspire current products.</span><span class='zh'>文章聚焦 DVD 菜单的设计美学，展示排版、动效与导航如何营造独特的用户体验。将旧有媒体界面的设计价值与现代 UX 联系起来，强调优秀设计对当代产品的启发作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rociiu/talorys" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Talorys – A self-hosted personal AI agent on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier</span><span class='zh'>Talorys：在 Cloudflare 免费层自托管的个人 AI 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription. It demonstrates how individuals can deploy private AI tools at the edge, balancing privacy, latency, and cost while avoiding vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。它展示了个人在边缘部署私有 AI 工具的可能性，平衡隐私、低延迟和成本，同时降低对单一厂商的依赖。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful project porting the original Doom to SQL showcases expressing game logic within a relational database.</span><span class='zh'>这个有趣的项目把原版 Doom 移植到 SQL，展示在关系型数据库中实现游戏逻辑的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful project porting the original Doom to SQL showcases expressing game logic within a relational database. It highlights the boundaries of SQL as a platform for non-database tasks and sparks discussion about creativity versus practicality in data-centric programming.</span><span class='zh'>这个有趣的项目把原版 Doom 移植到 SQL，展示在关系型数据库中实现游戏逻辑的可能性。它揭示了 SQL 作为非数据库任务平台的边界，并引发关于数据驱动编程中创造力与实用性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gleam.run/news/gleam-doesnt-compile-to-erlang-source-anymore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gleam doesn&#x27;t compile to Erlang source anymore</span><span class='zh'>Gleam 不再直接编译为 Erlang 源码</span></a><span class="dom">gleam.run</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Gleam compiler will no longer emit Erlang source code; it now targets BEAM bytecode directly.</span><span class='zh'>Gleam 编译器不再输出 Erlang 源码，而改为直接编译成 BEAM 字节码，减少对 Erlang 源码生成的依赖，简化工具链，并提升与 Elixir/Erlang 项目的互操作性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Gleam compiler will no longer emit Erlang source code; it now targets BEAM bytecode directly. This reduces dependency on Erlang source generation and streamlines toolchains, potentially improving interoperability with Elixir and Erlang projects, though it may alter existing code-generation workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Gleam 编译器不再输出 Erlang 源码，而改为直接编译成 BEAM 字节码，减少对 Erlang 源码生成的依赖，简化工具链，并提升与 Elixir/Erlang 项目的互操作性。此举可能影响现有的代码生成工作流。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>313</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975619' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>135</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49950554" data-id="49950554" data-tags="Programming" data-score="313" data-comments="328" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950554" data-time="1791087047">
<a class="thumb" href="https://nolanlawson.com/2026/10/03/why-dont-more-developers-use-the-platform/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://nolanlawson.com/_astro/dragndrop.CW1aBe6Q_ZDDLsQ.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nolanlawson.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">97</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nolanlawson.com/2026/10/03/why-dont-more-developers-use-the-platform/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why don&#x27;t more developers “use the platform”?</span><span class='zh'>为何没有更多开发者真正使用这个平台？</span></a><span class="dom">nolanlawson.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Developers often hesitate to use a platform despite its promises, due to fragmentation, weak tooling, or unclear incentives.</span><span class='zh'>尽管平台具备潜力，开发者往往因为生态碎片化、工具不足或激励不足而不愿真正采用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Developers often hesitate to use a platform despite its promises, due to fragmentation, weak tooling, or unclear incentives. The piece argues for a better developer experience, concrete migration paths, and compelling value to raise adoption.</span><span class='zh'>尽管平台具备潜力，开发者往往因为生态碎片化、工具不足或激励不足而不愿真正采用。文章提出应改善开发者体验、提供清晰的迁移路径，并创造更具吸引力的价值以提升接受度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress. The slides outline practical implications for proof, education, and research infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。讲义暗示对证明方法、教育和研究基础设施的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/mxc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MXC - a sandboxed code execution system</span><span class='zh'>MXC：一个沙箱化的代码执行系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MXC provides a sandboxed, policy-controlled code execution environment designed to run arbitrary code safely in the cloud.</span><span class='zh'>MXC 提供一个沙箱化、受策略控制的代码执行环境，旨在安全地在云端运行不受信任的代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MXC provides a sandboxed, policy-controlled code execution environment designed to run arbitrary code safely in the cloud. It supports isolating processes, controlling resources, and auditing behavior, enabling safer experimentation and evaluation of untrusted code.</span><span class='zh'>MXC 提供一个沙箱化、受策略控制的代码执行环境，旨在安全地在云端运行不受信任的代码。它通过进程隔离、资源限制和行为审计等机制，便于在云端进行安全的代码评估与试验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/M-Abozaid/esp32-c3-adblock" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ESP32-C3 Adblock</span><span class='zh'>ESP32-C3 广告拦截器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open-source project implements ad-blocking on ESP32-C3 devices, enabling users to run a lightweight network-wide blocker at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个面向 ESP32-C3 的开源广告拦截实现，能够在边缘设备上实现网络级拦截。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open-source project implements ad-blocking on ESP32-C3 devices, enabling users to run a lightweight network-wide blocker at the edge. The approach demonstrates how consumer IoT devices can contribute to privacy and performance by filtering ads and trackers before they reach devices.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个面向 ESP32-C3 的开源广告拦截实现，能够在边缘设备上实现网络级拦截。该方案展示了消费级物联网设备如何通过过滤广告与追踪来提升隐私与性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/eolix/photosuite/issues/77" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Photopea creator weighs in on Photosuite project</span><span class='zh'>Photopea 的创作者就 Photosuite 项目发表观点</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Photopea’s creator weighs in on the Photosuite project, likely voicing opinions on open formats, feature parity, and potential risks of competing web-based editors.</span><span class='zh'>Photopea 的创作者就 Photosuite 项目发表观点，涉及开放格式、功能对等与对等性风险等议题，提供对互操作性与用户期望的洞见。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Photopea’s creator weighs in on the Photosuite project, likely voicing opinions on open formats, feature parity, and potential risks of competing web-based editors. The note offers a vendor perspective on interoperability and user expectations.</span><span class='zh'>Photopea 的创作者就 Photosuite 项目发表观点，涉及开放格式、功能对等与对等性风险等议题，提供对互操作性与用户期望的洞见。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alex0ptr/once" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Once: Cache CLI commands</span><span class='zh'>Once：缓存 CLI 命令</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Once is a CLI utility that caches your frequently used shell commands for quick reuse.</span><span class='zh'>Once 是一个命令行工具，用于缓存常用命令以便快速重用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Once is a CLI utility that caches your frequently used shell commands for quick reuse. It speeds up repetitive tasks and improves reproducibility across sessions and machines. The tool targets developers and sysadmins who want smoother, repeatable workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Once 是一个命令行工具，用于缓存常用命令以便快速重用。它可加速重复任务并提升跨会话、跨机器的可重复性，适合开发者与系统管理员构建更顺畅的工作流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SamsungLabs/LittleBit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sub-1-Bit LLM Compression via Latent Factorization</span><span class='zh'>通过潜在因子分解实现 Sub-1-Bit 的大语言模型压缩</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LittleBit uses latent factorization to compress large language models to sub-1-bit representations, aiming to retain useful inference quality at far smaller sizes.</span><span class='zh'>LittleBit 通过潜在因子分解把大模型压缩到低于1比特的表示，意在在显著减小模型规模的同时维持可用的推理质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LittleBit uses latent factorization to compress large language models to sub-1-bit representations, aiming to retain useful inference quality at far smaller sizes. The approach could unlock running capable LLMs on edge devices and specialized accelerators, expanding deployment scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>LittleBit 通过潜在因子分解把大模型压缩到低于1比特的表示，意在在显著减小模型规模的同时维持可用的推理质量。该方法有望让边缘设备和定制加速器也能部署强大的 LLM，拓展应用场景。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project porting the TypeScript toolchain to Rust with the help of a large language model, aiming for faster compilation and stronger safety guarantees. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks.</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks. The finding underscores the importance of verifying all certificate handling in proxy software and applying patches.</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。这一发现强调了代理软件中对证书处理的严格验证和及时打补丁的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dfd-tud/deda" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DEDA – Tracking Dots Extraction, Decoding and Anonymisation Toolkit</span><span class='zh'>DEDA：跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化工具包</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DEDA provides a toolkit for extracting, decoding, and anonymizing tracking dots, enabling researchers to study marker-based tracking and improve privacy protections.</span><span class='zh'>DEDA 提供一个跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化的工具包，帮助研究者研究基于标记的跟踪并提升隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DEDA provides a toolkit for extracting, decoding, and anonymizing tracking dots, enabling researchers to study marker-based tracking and improve privacy protections. It highlights practical methods for analyzing and mitigating telemetry signals.</span><span class='zh'>DEDA 提供一个跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化的工具包，帮助研究者研究基于标记的跟踪并提升隐私保护。它展示了分析与缓解遥测信号的实际方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/explaindb/explaindb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExplainDB: A Database System Built for Understandability</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB：为可理解性而生的数据库系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExplainDB is a database system designed with understandability in mind, likely offering transparent query planning and explainability features to help developers and operators reason about data flows.</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB 是以可理解性为设计目标的数据库系统，可能提供透明的查询规划和可解释性特性，帮助开发者与运维更清晰地理解数据流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExplainDB is a database system designed with understandability in mind, likely offering transparent query planning and explainability features to help developers and operators reason about data flows.</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB 是以可理解性为设计目标的数据库系统，可能提供透明的查询规划和可解释性特性，帮助开发者与运维更清晰地理解数据流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/p10node/k10s" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: K10s – A Clickable Kubernetes TUI (Go, Bubble Tea)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：K10s——一个可点击的 Kubernetes TUI（Go、Bubble Tea）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>K10s is a clickable terminal UI for Kubernetes built with Go and Bubble Tea, enabling point-and-click operations inside a TUI instead of pure command lines.</span><span class='zh'>K10s 是一个用 Go 和 Bubble Tea 构建的可点击 Kubernetes 终端界面，允许在 TUI 内进行点选操作而非仅用命令行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>K10s is a clickable terminal UI for Kubernetes built with Go and Bubble Tea, enabling point-and-click operations inside a TUI instead of pure command lines. By combining a familiar CLI domain with a UI that can be navigated via keyboard or mouse, it aims to reduce the cognitive load of managing clusters in the terminal. This project highlights how terminal interfaces can augment traditional DevOps tooling without sacrificing portability.</span><span class='zh'>K10s 是一个用 Go 和 Bubble Tea 构建的可点击 Kubernetes 终端界面，允许在 TUI 内进行点选操作而非仅用命令行。它将熟悉的命令行领域与可键盘/鼠标导航的 UI 结合，旨在降低终端管理集群的认知负担。该项目展示了终端工具如何在不牺牲可移植性的前提下变得更易用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</span><span class='zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/moritzwilksch/diffle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Diffle, a keyboard-centric diff viewer with LSP support</span><span class='zh'>Diffle：以键盘为中心的 Diff 查看器，支持 LSP</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Diffle is a diff viewer designed for keyboard-first navigation with added LSP support for code-aware diffs.</span><span class='zh'>Diffle 是一个以键盘优先为设计的差异查看工具，并集成了对 LSP 的支持，提升对 diffs 的代码感知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Diffle is a diff viewer designed for keyboard-first navigation with added LSP support for code-aware diffs. It aims to speed up reviews and patch creation by minimizing mouse use and providing inline intelligence. Takeaway: keyboard-centric tools with code intelligence can accelerate code comparison workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Diffle 是一个以键盘优先为设计的差异查看工具，并集成了对 LSP 的支持，提升对 diffs 的代码感知。通过最小化鼠标使用并提供代码智能，能加速审查和补丁编写。要点是键盘优先的工具结合代码智能能提升效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rgpu is a PyTorch device where tensors live on a remote GPU, enabling offloading and potential speedups for large models.</span><span class='zh'>Rgpu 是一个让张量驻留在远程 GPU 的 PyTorch 设备，支持将计算分发到另一台主机以进行加速。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rgpu is a PyTorch device where tensors live on a remote GPU, enabling offloading and potential speedups for large models. It highlights tradeoffs in latency, bandwidth, and distributed computation.</span><span class='zh'>Rgpu 是一个让张量驻留在远程 GPU 的 PyTorch 设备，支持将计算分发到另一台主机以进行加速。它揭示了延迟、带宽与分布式计算的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/ethical-foss/open-slopware" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>open-slopware – Alternatives to FOSS projects choosing to use LLMs/AI</span><span class='zh'>open-slopware：在选择使用 LLM/AI 的 FOSS 项目中的替代方案</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>open-slopware explores options for FOSS projects that adopt LLMs/AI, weighing openness, licensing, and governance implications.</span><span class='zh'>open-slopware 探讨在采用大型语言模型/人工智能的 FOSS 项目中可以采用的替代路径，权衡开放性、许可与治理的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>open-slopware explores options for FOSS projects that adopt LLMs/AI, weighing openness, licensing, and governance implications. It highlights how AI integration can complicate traditional OSS philosophies.</span><span class='zh'>open-slopware 探讨在采用大型语言模型/人工智能的 FOSS 项目中可以采用的替代路径，权衡开放性、许可与治理的影响。强调 AI 集成如何给传统开源理念带来新的挑战。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">503</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lzprograma/Chip-Hexa" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chip-hex:cost-benefit, energy efficiency and hexagonal</span><span class='zh'>Chip-Hexa：成本、能效与六边形布局</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chip-Hexa surveys the potential benefits of hexagonal chip layouts, arguing that hex tiling can improve interconnect density, energy efficiency, and cooling compared with traditional square grids.</span><span class='zh'>Chip-Hexa 探讨六边形芯片布局在成本、能耗与互连密度方面的潜在优势，比较六边形与传统方格的取舍，提出未来芯片架构的设计思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chip-Hexa surveys the potential benefits of hexagonal chip layouts, arguing that hex tiling can improve interconnect density, energy efficiency, and cooling compared with traditional square grids. It weighs design and fabrication trade-offs, offering ideas for future chip architectures.</span><span class='zh'>Chip-Hexa 探讨六边形芯片布局在成本、能耗与互连密度方面的潜在优势，比较六边形与传统方格的取舍，提出未来芯片架构的设计思路。</span></p>
